1106_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:1106
			exists = character:167925
		}
		character:1106 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:167925 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

		limit = {
			exists = character:167683
			exists = character:32120
		}
		character:167683 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:32120 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
